```solidity
    bool lockFlag = true;
    mapping(address => bool) locked;
    mapping(address => bool) exclude;

    function enableTransfer(bool _enable) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        transferEnabled = _enable;
    }

    function disableLock(bool _enable) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        lockFlag = _enable;
        return true;
    }

    function addLock(address _addr) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_addr != msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked[_addr] = true;
        return true;
    }

    function setExclude(address _addr) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        exclude[_addr] = true;
        return true;
    }

    function removeLock(address _addr) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked[_addr] = false;
        return true;
    }

    modifier transferAllowed(address _addr) {
        if (!exclude[_addr]) {
            if (!(transferEnabled)) {
                revert();

            if (lockFlag) {

                    revert();
                }
            }
        }

    }

contract SMT is StandardToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_SMT(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function() public {
        revert();
    }

    string public name = "SmartMesh Token";
    uint8 public decimals = 18;
    string public symbol = "SMT";
    string public version = "v0.1";
    uint256 public allocateEndTime;
    mapping(address => uint256) nonces;

    constructor() public {
        allocateEndTime = now + 1 days;
    }

    function transferProxy(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value, uint256 _feeSmt, uint8 _v, bytes32 _r, bytes32 _s) public returns (bool) {
        if (balances[_from] < _feeSmt + _value || _feeSmt > _feeSmt + _value) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 nonce = nonces[_from];
        bytes32 h = keccak256(_from, _to, _value, _feeSmt, nonce, address(this));
        if (_from != ecrecover(h, _v, _r, _s)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances[_to] + _value < balances[_to] || balances[msg.sender] + _feeSmt < balances[msg.sender]) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[_to] += _value;
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        balances[msg.sender] += _feeSmt;
        emit Transfer(_from, msg.sender, _feeSmt);
        balances[_from] -= _value + _feeSmt;
        nonces[_from] = nonce + 1;
        return true;
    }

    function approveProxy(address _from, address _spender, uint256 _value, uint8 _v, bytes32 _r, bytes32 _s) public returns (bool success) {
        uint256 nonce = nonces[_from];
        bytes32 hash = keccak256(_from, _spender, _value, nonce, address(this));
        if (_from != ecrecover(hash, _v, _r, _s)) {
            revert();
        }
        allowed[_from][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(_from, _spender, _value);
        nonces[_from] = nonce + 1;
        return true;
    }

    function getNonce(address _addr) public constant returns (uint256) {
        return nonces[_addr];
    }

    function allocateTokens(address[] _owners, uint256[] _values) public {
        if (allocateEndTime < now) {
            revert();
        }
        if (_owners.length != _values.length) {
            revert();
        }
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _owners.length; i++) {
            address to = _owners[i];
            uint256 value = _values[i];
            if (totalSupply + value <= totalSupply || balances[to] + value <= balances[to]) {
                revert();
            }
            totalSupply += value;
            balances[to] += value;
        }
    }
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.24;

contract ERC20 {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ERC20(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function totalSupply() public view returns (uint256);

    function balanceOf(address who) public view returns (uint256);

    function transfer(address to, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);

    function allowance(address owner, address spender) public view returns (uint256);

    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    function approve(address spender, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);
}

contract Lockable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Lockable(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    bool public tokenTransfer;
    address public owner;
    mapping(address => bool) public unlockAddress;
    mapping(address => bool) public lockAddress;
    event Locked(address lockAddress, bool status);
    event Unlocked(address unlockedAddress, bool status);
    modifier isTokenTransfer() {
        if (!tokenTransfer) {
            if (!(unlockAddress[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
        }
        _;
    }
    modifier checkLock() {
        if (!(!lockAddress[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    modifier isOwner() {
        if (!(owner == msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    constructor() public {
        tokenTransfer = false;
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    function setLockAddress(address target, bool status) external isOwner {
        if (!(owner == msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(owner != target)) {
            revert();
        }
        lockAddress[target] = status;
        emit Locked(target, status);
    }

    function setUnlockAddress(address target, bool status) external isOwner {
        if (!(owner == msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        unlockAddress[target] = status;
        emit Unlocked(target, status);
    }
}

library SafeMath {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_SafeMath(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function mul(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256 c) {
        if (a == 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        c = a * b;
        if (!(c / a == b)) {
            revert();
        }
        return c;
    }

    function div(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
        return a / b;
    }

    function sub(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
        if (!(b <= a)) {
            revert();
        }
        return a - b;
    }

    function add(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256 c) {
        c = a + b;
        if (!(c >= a)) {
            revert();
        }
        return c;
    }
}

contract YeedToken is ERC20, Lockable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_YeedToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    string public constant name = "YGGDRASH";
    string public constant symbol = "YEED";
    uint8 public constant decimals = 18;
    bool public adminMode;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    mapping(address => uint256) internal _balances;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) internal _approvals;
    uint256 internal _supply;
    event TokenBurned(address burnAddress, uint256 amountOfTokens);
    event SetTokenTransfer(bool transfer);
    event SetAdminMode(bool adminMode);
    event EmergencyTransfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);
    modifier isAdminMode() {
        if (!(adminMode)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    constructor(uint256 initial_balance) public {
        if (!(initial_balance != 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        _supply = initial_balance;
        _balances[msg.sender] = initial_balance;
        emit Transfer(address(0x0), msg.sender, initial_balance);
    }

    function totalSupply() public view returns (uint256) {
        return _supply;
    }

    function balanceOf(address who) public view returns (uint256) {
        return _balances[who];
    }

    function transfer(address to, uint256 value) public isTokenTransfer checkLock returns (bool) {
        if (!tokenTransfer) {
            if (!(unlockAddress[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
        }
        if (!(!lockAddress[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_balances[msg.sender] >= value)) {
            revert();
        }
        _balances[msg.sender] = _balances[msg.sender].sub(value);
        _balances[to] = _balances[to].add(value);
        emit Transfer(msg.sender, to, value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address owner, address spender) public view returns (uint256) {
        return _approvals[owner][spender];
    }

    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 value) public isTokenTransfer checkLock returns (bool success) {
        if (!tokenTransfer) {
            if (!(unlockAddress[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
        }
        if (!(!lockAddress[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!lockAddress[from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_balances[from] >= value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_approvals[from][msg.sender] >= value)) {
            revert();
        }
        _balances[from] = _balances[from].sub(value);
        _balances[to] = _balances[to].add(value);
        _approvals[from][msg.sender] = _approvals[from][msg.sender].sub(value);
        emit Transfer(from, to, value);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address spender, uint256 value) public checkLock returns (bool) {
        if (!(!lockAddress[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        _approvals[msg.sender][spender] = value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, spender, value);
        return true;
    }

    function increaseApproval(address _spender, uint256 _addedValue) public checkLock returns (bool) {
        if (!(!lockAddress[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        _approvals[msg.sender][_spender] = (_approvals[msg.sender][_spender].add(_addedValue));
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract ACCURAL_DEPOSIT {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ACCURAL_DEPOSIT(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) public balances;
    uint public MinSum = 1 ether;
    LogFile Log = LogFile(0x0486cF65A2F2F3A392CBEa398AFB7F5f0B72FF46);
    bool intitalized;

    function SetMinSum(uint _val) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            revert();
        }
        MinSum = _val;
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    uint256 totalSupply = 100000;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function destroy(address _from, uint256 _amount) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[_from] -= _amount;
        totalSupply -= _amount;
    }

    function SetLogFile(address _log) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            revert();
        }
        Log = LogFile(_log);
    }

    function Initialized() public {
        intitalized = true;
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
        Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Put");
    }

    function Collect(uint _am) public payable {
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= MinSum && balances[msg.sender] >= _am) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "Collect");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {
        {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Put");
        }
    }
}

contract LogFile {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_LogFile(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    struct Message {
        address Sender;
        string Data;
        uint Val;
        uint Time;
    }
    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
    bool public presaleFinalized = false;
    bool public crowdfundFinalized = false;
    event CrowdfundFinalized(uint tokensRemaining);
    event PresaleFinalized(uint tokensRemaining);
    modifier notBeforeCrowdfundEnds() {
        if (!(now >= crowdfundEndsAt)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    modifier checkVanbexTeamVestingPeriod() {
        if (!(now >= vanbexTeamVestingPeriod)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    modifier onlyCrowdfund() {
        if (!(msg.sender == crowdfundAddress)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _amount) notBeforeCrowdfundEnds returns (bool success) {
        if (!(now >= crowdfundEndsAt)) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_79_0_0;
        {
            {
                FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_79_0_0 = balances[msg.sender];
            }
        }
        if (!(FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_79_0_0 >= _amount)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_amount);
        }

        {
            balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_amount);
        }

        Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _amount);
        return true;
    }

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) notBeforeCrowdfundEnds returns (bool success) {
        if (!(now >= crowdfundEndsAt)) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 FuelToken_allowance_TMP_90_0_0;
        {
            {
                FuelToken_allowance_TMP_90_0_0 = allowed[_from][msg.sender];
            }
        }
        if (!(FuelToken_allowance_TMP_90_0_0 >= _amount)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_amount);
        }

        {
            balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_amount);
        }

        allowed[_from][msg.sender] = allowed[_from][msg.sender].sub(_amount);
        Transfer(_from, _to, _amount);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        uint256 FuelToken_allowance_TMP_100_0_0;
        {
            {
                FuelToken_allowance_TMP_100_0_0 = allowed[msg.sender][_spender];
            }
        }
        if (!((_value == 0) || (FuelToken_allowance_TMP_100_0_0 == 0))) {
            revert();
        }
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) constant returns (uint256 remaining) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) constant returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function FuelToken() {
        crowdfundEndsAt = 1509292800;
        vanbexTeamVestingPeriod = crowdfundEndsAt.add(183 * 1 days);
        totalSupply = 1 * 10 ** 27;
        vanbexTeamSupply = 5 * 10 ** 25;
        platformSupply = 5 * 10 ** 25;
        incentivisingEffortsSupply = 1 * 10 ** 26;
        presaleSupply = 54 * 10 ** 25;
        icoSupply = 26 * 10 ** 25;
        presaleAmountRemaining = presaleSupply;
        vanbexTeamAddress = 0xCF701D8eA4C727466D42651dda127c0c033076B0;
        platformAddress = 0xF5b5f6c1E233671B220C2A19Af10Fd18785D0744;
        incentivisingEffortsAddress = 0x5584b17B40F6a2E412e65FcB1533f39Fc7D8Aa26;
        {
            balances[incentivisingEffortsAddress] = balances[incentivisingEffortsAddress].add(incentivisingEffortsSupply);
        }

        {
            balances[platformAddress] = balances[platformAddress].add(platformSupply);
        }
    }

    function setCrowdfundAddress(address _crowdfundAddress) external onlyOwner nonZeroAddress(_crowdfundAddress) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_crowdfundAddress != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(crowdfundAddress == address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        crowdfundAddress = _crowdfundAddress;
        {
            balances[crowdfundAddress] = balances[crowdfundAddress].add(icoSupply);
        }
    }

    function transferFromCrowdfund(address _to, uint256 _amount) onlyCrowdfund nonZeroAmount(_amount) nonZeroAddress(_to) returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == crowdfundAddress)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_amount > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_146_0_0;
        {
            {
                FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_146_0_0 = balances[crowdfundAddress];
            }
        }
        if (!(FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_146_0_0 >= _amount)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            balances[crowdfundAddress] = balances[crowdfundAddress].sub(_amount);
        }

        {
            balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_amount);
        }

        Transfer(address(0x0), _to, _amount);
        return true;
    }

    function releaseVanbexTeamTokens() checkVanbexTeamVestingPeriod onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(now >= vanbexTeamVestingPeriod)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(vanbexTeamSupply > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            balances[vanbexTeamAddress] = balances[vanbexTeamAddress].add(vanbexTeamSupply);
        }

        Transfer(address(0x0), vanbexTeamAddress, vanbexTeamSupply);
        vanbexTeamSupply = 0;
        return true;
    }

    function finalizePresale() external onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(presaleFinalized == false)) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 amount = presaleAmountRemaining;
        if (amount != 0) {
            presaleAmountRemaining = 0;
            {
                balances[crowdfundAddress] = balances[crowdfundAddress].add(amount);
            }
        }
        presaleFinalized = true;
        PresaleFinalized(amount);
        return true;
    }

    function finalizeCrowdfund() external onlyCrowdfund {
        if (!(msg.sender == crowdfundAddress)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(presaleFinalized == true && crowdfundFinalized == false)) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_189_0_0;
        {
            {
                FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_189_0_0 = balances[crowdfundAddress];
            }
        }
        uint256 amount = FuelToken_balanceOf_TMP_189_0_0;
        if (amount > 0) {
            balances[crowdfundAddress] = 0;
            {
                balances[platformAddress] = balances[platformAddress].add(amount);
            }

            Transfer(crowdfundAddress, platformAddress, amount);
        }
        crowdfundFinalized = true;
        CrowdfundFinalized(amount);
    }

    function deliverPresaleFuelBalances(address[] _batchOfAddresses, uint[] _amountOfFuel) external onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _batchOfAddresses.length; i++) {
            {
                address REF_14_0_0 = _batchOfAddresses[i];
                uint256 REF_15_0_0 = _amountOfFuel[i];
                if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
                    revert();
                }
                if (!(presaleAmountRemaining > 0)) {
                    revert();
                }
                {
                    balances[REF_14_0_0] = balances[REF_14_0_0].add(REF_15_0_0);
                }

                Transfer(address(0x0), REF_14_0_0, REF_15_0_0);
                presaleAmountRemaining = presaleAmountRemaining.sub(REF_15_0_0);
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    function deliverPresaleFuelBalance(address _accountHolder, uint _amountOfBoughtFuel) internal onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(presaleAmountRemaining > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            balances[_accountHolder] = balances[_accountHolder].add(_amountOfBoughtFuel);
        }

        Transfer(address(0x0), _accountHolder, _amountOfBoughtFuel);
        presaleAmountRemaining = presaleAmountRemaining.sub(_amountOfBoughtFuel);
    }

    function addToBalance(address _address, uint _amount) internal {
        balances[_address] = balances[_address].add(_amount);
    }

    function decrementBalance(address _address, uint _amount) internal {
        balances[_address] = balances[_address].sub(_amount);
    }
}
```

SHA-256 `e79dde0d79edb61e93409f14765a11deebfbecbd91a98d051a501375546fa7d2`

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.21;

contract TokenSaleChallenge {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_TokenSaleChallenge(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;
    uint256 constant PRICE_PER_TOKEN = 1 ether;

    function TokenSaleChallenge(address _player) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == 1 ether)) {
            revert();
        }
    }

    function isComplete() public view returns (bool) {
        return address(this).balance < 1 ether;
    }

    function buy(uint256 numTokens) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == numTokens * PRICE_PER_TOKEN)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[msg.sender] += numTokens;
    }

    function sell(uint256 numTokens) public {
        if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= numTokens)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[msg.sender] -= numTokens;
        msg.sender.transfer(numTokens * PRICE_PER_TOKEN);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    uint256 totalSupply = 100000;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function destroy(address _from, uint256 _amount) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[_from] -= _amount;
        totalSupply -= _amount;
    }
}
```
